TASK

Evaluate what the assigned experimental sources establish about the reference molecule and its possible function. Keep the actual caller question, supplied material, scientific scope and constraints below as your job. A function can emerge from the evidence; an initial annotation or public searchable name is not required. Decide which uncertainty is useful to resolve next, choose among the permitted source operations and literature investigations, and revise your interpretation using actual outcomes.

Establish which organism, genotype, molecule, construct, preparation and experimental system each result concerns. Distinguish the source organism from an expression host, a tagged construct from a native molecule, and a reference preparation from the supplied annotation target. Preserve the actual measured entity, endpoint, units, normalization, comparison numerator and denominator, conditions, intended experimental change and controls. A shared name, source paper or equal number does not establish a common experiment or molecular identity.

Judge the bearing of the measured endpoint on the specific functional assertion. Molecular conversion, transport and binding experiments answer different questions from growth, survival or other perturbation/restoration phenotypes. A recovery-growth result is not an enzyme activity measurement. A band-signal ratio is not calibrated molar or active protein amount. A no-inhibitor reference is not a no-enzyme blank. Oxygen consumption does not itself identify product formation or a quantitative productive coupling ratio. Keep the source's observation separate from the authors' inference and from your interpretation.

Keep experimental comparability outside the deliberately tested difference separate from isolation of the measured effect. Ask which amounts or conditions could explain a comparison, whether molecules replace one another or coexist, and which contributors remain in a mixed readout. Similar conditions do not prove attribution. A complementary experiment can address a named weakness without becoming independent replication. Preserve contradictions and source-representation conflicts; measurements on different endpoints or conditions are not automatically inconsistent repeats.

When a narrower literature investigation is useful, define its actual question, source scope, constraints and requested typed information. Ask for the numerical components and experimental relationships needed for your next decision, including relevant uncertainty, controls, unavailable reasons and source identity. Use the declared return projections to construct a closed form; retain actual reference meanings and select only the needed components. Children own their iterative source search, reading and interpretation choices. Their permitted relationships are alternatives, not a required itinerary, reading quota or automatic sequence.

Integrate only information actually received through your own outcomes or requested admitted child forms. A globally stored source or a reference needed for validation is not delivered scientific knowledge. Read scoped evidence when needed. Preserve units, bounds, uncertainty definitions, experimental meaning and dependence when comparing results. Missing values remain unknown; nondetection is not exact zero. Do not interchange SD, SEM, confidence intervals or untyped error bars. Convert them only when the required statistical information and assumptions support the calculation. Do not count technical repetitions as independent experiments or repeated representations as new evidence. Models identify source meanings and supported operands; deterministic operations perform parsing, validation, unit conversion and arithmetic.

A numerical source-effect component evaluates its declared experiment and endpoint. It is not a probability of function, target annotation support or investigation-progress credit. Source findings may qualify or weaken a proposed function while still being useful information. Sequence/structural correspondence and later combination into annotation credit remain separate work. Do not invent target-transfer weights, reliability categories, missing controls, covariance or scientific progress. Do not manufacture target assessments merely to give source processing credit or hasten stopping.

Retain valid partial results and actionable source limitations. Your task remains this parent investigation after a child return; the admitted return is evidence for your next decision, not another task prompt. Use ordinary reasoning to integrate findings and update only your declared typed report when one is requested. Do not add a second model call to restate recorded facts or compose a report. Source navigation, bookkeeping and report construction add no scientific contribution by themselves. The configured controller owns continuation; there is no source-completion gate or fixed action count.

When requesting saved numerical experimental components listed as available recorded return sources, choose a top-level return field whose declaration contains only $ref with the exact value episode-fact:<source>, using that source's declared name. The host expands this explicitly selected declaration to the original complete closed type before commissioning; the numerical sheet itself remains with the host. Choose the needed components through the child assignment. Keep bound fact fields out of initial_report_json; the runtime fills them from saved results. Use separate constrained fields for interpretations and unresolved questions, preserving the parent's requested meanings. Do not ask the child to rewrite saved numerical cells as model-authored report values.

Population operations available within literature children

| Child operation | Input capability and meaning |
| --- | --- |
| literature.rate.populate | Populate source-preparation rate cells from a selected already acquired CSV/TSV summary. Preserve actual subjects, conditions, units, uncertainty and missingness. This finite table route does not read arbitrary prose/figures or calculate target transfer/annotation credit. Select a statistical model only when supported; otherwise use none and unknown dependence. |
| literature.rate.observations.populate | Populate requested numerical components from actually received source XLSX cells. The original row-labelled branch retains reported-entry descriptive contrasts under its original layout and native convention. The explicit reported-curve branch reads concentration rows and reporting columns, authenticates selected unit/endpoint/sampling context, and supplies requested descriptive spread, fitted-estimator and conditional covariance scalars. Keep the mean of fitted parameter vectors separate from fitting point means. Source labels do not establish physical IID preparations. Preserve fixed concentration-coordinate scope, endpoint conflicts, source versus implementation limitations and available-only partials. Native amount-rate Vmax is not kcat; no overall reliability grade or annotation credit. |
| literature.calibration.recovery.populate | Populate requested source-bound recovery-control components from exact original numerical/unit spans. Preserve measured/expected roles, reporter-standard versus enzyme-activity-spike meaning, matrix, endpoint, blank treatment, actual SD/SEM meaning and original measurement reuse. Calculate recovery, signed bias and reported-versus-displayed discrepancy deterministically. Unknown inputs preserve partials. This control does not establish product identity, independent verification or applicability to another measurement; no target correction or annotation credit is supplied. |
| literature.product_coupling.populate | Populate requested A105 source product/cofactor components from exact received number/unit spans and witnessed endpoint, pairing and reaction-account meanings. Keep native product/cofactor/reported-percent quantities separate, descriptive product-per-cofactor ratio distinct from a supported physical productive fraction, and molecule-specific attribution unresolved when unknown. No default or fitted stoichiometry, nondetection-as-zero ratio, invented covariance, independent reconstruction, target transfer or annotation credit. Uncertainty requires the explicitly owned joint covariance of selected rate estimates; requested derived returns contain no unrequested native operands or matrix. |
| literature.measurement_validation.populate | Populate requested source precision and control-separation components from exact CV/Z-prime number, label, unit and source-meaning witnesses, optionally using only actually possessed recovery/bias rows. Preserve each within/between population separately, unknown denominator relationships, conditional arithmetic, original measurement reuse and proposed-versus-reported meanings. Native statistics need no prior framework sheet. These components are not product identity, independent verification, a function probability, target transfer or final annotation credit. |
| literature.rate.replicates.populate | Read explicitly mapped retained raw-rate CSV/TSV measurements from an actually received source. Select exact subject/experiment and reference/test groups, unit model and dependence; source bytes supply all measured numbers. Technical readings first average within actual units, then unit means receive equal weight. Preserve actual measurement/unit counts, sampling bases, duplicate/conflicting identities and missing/censored partials. SD is descriptive; SE is conditional on the selected design. This finite raw-table operation does not parse arbitrary publisher layouts or supply assay specificity, error rates, target transfer or annotation credit. |
| literature.quantity.populate | Read one literal specific activity or apparent Km from selected original JATS prose, or apparent Km from selected original HTML prose. Select quantity role and analyte, not numbers. HTML paragraphs without IDs use the explicit direct_paragraph:N selector within the selected section. Preserve untyped plus-minus spread. Subject-token presence does not establish experimental attribution; inspect the assay context separately. No comparison arms, conditions, product identity or missing correction score are invented. |
| literature.assay.populate | Interpret the source assay's actual subject, conditions, endpoint and molecular arrangement with exact source witnesses. Select reported numerical spans only when they have the stated correction-operand meanings. Deterministically parse and correct the interpreted source endpoint, preserving full covariance, conditional assumptions and unknowns. Exact quotes validate custody, not interpretation; no target transfer or annotation probability. |
| literature.ratio.populate | Read reported relative-signal percentages from selected JATS prose and optionally compare retained visual transcriptions with original image custody. Preserve the actual denominator/arrangement and conflicting candidate values. Signal is not calibrated molar amount or an isolated functional effect; no source reliability or target-transfer score is fabricated. |
| literature.binding.populate | Populate source binding-parameter components from an already received table or explicitly retained visual transcription. Keep IC50 distinct from reported Hill-fit Kd, actual protein/state/ligand identities, reported fit-spread basis and point/limit arithmetic. Original image custody is validated; no OCR, sampling confidence, intrinsic-affinity conversion or target credit is inferred. |
| literature.binding.model.populate | Evaluate the ordered parameter contrast in an actually received committed binding sheet using an explicitly supplied source model. Preserve full log-covariance measurement ownership, known versus estimated variance, and the stated threshold-reporting mechanism. Missing model source retains usable native contrast and explicit unknown likelihood cells. No inference of covariance from fit spreads or counts, limit-as-point calculation, intrinsic-affinity conversion, target transfer or annotation credit. |
| literature.transport.response.populate | Calculate a conditional source substrate-response correction from exact concentration spans and a received saved apparent-Km quantity. Preserve actual molecule, preparation, analyte, endpoint and fitted-curve contexts. Different or unknown curves leave the comparison unavailable. Full covariance retains physical owners and shared Km once; no sampling variance from untyped spread, target transfer or annotation credit is inferred. |
| literature.perturbation.populate | Populate reference perturbation/restoration components from source-bound design interpretations and optional received quantitative data. Keep actual recipient, changed component, partner, endpoint calibration and real restoration arms distinct. Qualitative-only outcomes cannot supply numerical rates. Preserve nuisance comparability separately from effect isolation and leave missing inputs/uncertainty unknown. No target transfer, necessity inference from an unmeasured catalytic knockout, or final annotation credit. |
| literature.structure.populate | Populate source-model geometry and local residue RSCC components from received coordinates and optional matching deposited validation. Select exact model/author atom groups and fixed physical criteria. Preserve missing endpoints, validation entry/revision mismatch and unreported coordinate uncertainty. The explicit scope is conditional deposited-model geometry; it does not establish population occupancy, causal mechanism, target correspondence or annotation credit. |
| literature.reaction.populate | Populate reported reference reaction-ratio residuals, named-prediction comparisons and simple resource-budget components from an explicitly retained received dataset or native source-selection recipe. Read exact report ratio points or author a:b expressions, summary uncertainty/counts and simple resource quantities with source units. Preserve unknown replication, missing alternatives, calibration basis and incomplete resource constraints. Product-pair ratios, instrument signals, supplied reagent budgets and usable capacity have distinct meanings. This route does not parse NMR spectra, establish measured oxygen coupling, apply missing corrections or calculate target transfer/annotation credit. |
| literature.context.populate | Populate biological context compatibility from source-bound retained observations and domain/route meanings. Keep organism capability distinct from fixed-sample coverage; preserve physical unit bases, missing local mapping, unexamined states, sample weights, pairing and conditional sampling design. Logical domain bounds are not confidence intervals or intuitive quality grades. Purified-enzyme assays are not automatically organism witnesses or habitat samples. No target correspondence or annotation credit is calculated. |
| literature.figure.populate | Read native experimental mean-bar ordinates from received original images using a retained linear-axis selection recipe. Preserve both subject/partner arms, endpoint and normalization meanings, source representation relationships, reported repeat/spread meanings and conditional digitization bounds. Return raw ordinates and a signed difference; pixel extraction does not supply SD, statistical significance, molar calibration, partner necessity or target credit. |
| literature.partner.populate | Populate source experimental partner-removal and addback components from received retained measurements and exact source witnesses. Preserve directed molecule/partner roles, nitrogen and other nonpartner conditions, normalization identity, physical measurement identity and reported spread meanings. Incompatible or incomplete comparisons keep native values and explicit unknown components. Censor domains are not confidence intervals; chemical bypass and partner swaps do not establish universal partner necessity, genomic presence, target transfer or annotation credit. |
| literature.partner.source.populate | Populate the same experimental partner components directly from selected literal source spans. Select complete rate/limit tokens, native units, spread meanings and source-bound entity/physical-measurement identities; do not supply scientific numbers or a prepared numeric JSON dataset. For source-defined activity units such as U/mg, select the endpoint-specific definition, quantity, molecular entity and time basis; no universal U conversion is assumed. Deterministic readers preserve original tokens, convert supported rate units and retain unknown normalization, conditions or observation identity. A repeated representation is not a new measurement. No sampling uncertainty, universal necessity, target correspondence or annotation credit is inferred. |
| literature.rate.source.populate | Populate the existing source-rate comparison sheet directly from selected literal paper spans, without a prepared CSV/TSV or model-authored scientific numbers. Request literature_rate_source_domain_cells for full reported-operand domains, including finite two-sided enclosures; the original literature_rate_source_cells projection preserves each arm's subject, construct, preparation, approximation and one-sided limits; literature_rate_cells is the separate legacy table projection. Select source-backed endpoint/basis relationships and statistical assumptions; different constructs may be the intended comparison. Source-defined U/mg requires its endpoint-specific definition. Missing meanings, unsupported units and censored values retain partial inputs. Exact witnesses verify custody, not causal isolation, target correspondence or annotation credit. |
| literature.amount.populate | Populate A105 source amount components from exact reported numerical/unit spans and witnessed experimental meanings. Keep target/total same-stock mass-fraction estimates separate from nominal molar amounts between arms. Preserve both quantity roles, preparations, methods, bases, bounds, approximation and source-owned covariance. Ratios above one and missing relationships remain explicit; no active fraction, universal activity correction, target transfer or annotation credit is inferred. The conditional mass-denominator factor is retained without applying it to a saved rate. |
| literature.optical.populate | Populate the existing net_rate and variance_net_rate input meanings from selected source-bound optical observations. Preserve NADH-depletion endpoint scope, actual subject/construct/preparation/conditions, qualified or missing inputs and source-owned covariance. Optical epsilon/path conversion is not recovery k, protein amount, product attribution or a complete activity score. Repeated representations create no independent evidence or annotation credit. |
| literature.kinetic.populate | Read source-bound reported Km, kcat or catalytic-efficiency estimates and populate native parameter, difference, ratio and log-contrast components for separately assayed substrates. Preserve actual molecule/construct, per-row protocols, qualifications and source uncertainty meanings. This native route has no covariance/model source reader; model-dependent evidence cells are not supported here, which is not a finding that the paper lacks covariance. No quotient of reported means, common-condition correction, intrinsic-affinity inference, target transfer or annotation credit. |
| literature.kinetic.model.populate | Populate the existing four conditional kinetic log-model cells from an actually received committed native kinetic sheet and an optional received retained model source. Model JSON uses a105_kinetic_log_model_v1 fields except source; provenance is bound from actual bytes. Preserve exact numerator/denominator physical estimate owners, natural-log canonical-parameter basis, full covariance, known/estimated/unknown variance and source interpretation witnesses. Reported SD/SEM/counts never supply covariance. These dependent parameter-contrast statistics are not assay reliability, function probabilities, target transfer or annotation credit. |
| literature.binding.interaction.populate | Read four selected original-image binding cells for two separately assayed source molecular forms and two ligands. Populate native IC50 or reported Hill-fit Kd parameters, within-form ligand ratios and their interaction/log interaction. Preserve actual source meanings, physical-fit identity, uncertain or conflicting readings, units, limits and partial uncertainty; unknown common kind retains raw results without choosing a default. Covariance/model source reading is unimplemented here, not a finding about the paper. No intrinsic-affinity conversion, target transfer or annotation credit. |
| literature.binding.interaction.model.populate | Populate the existing four conditional binding interaction log-model cells from an actually received committed native interaction sheet and an optional explicit received model packet. The prepared binding_interaction_log_model_v1 JSON omits source; provenance is bound from actual bytes. Keep four exact physical fit owners and canonical estimate keys, natural-log nM basis, full covariance, known/estimated/unknown status and source-defined reporting-event support. No paper-covariance packet producer is supplied here. SD/SEM/counts and distinct preparations do not establish covariance or independence. These dependent parameter-interaction statistics are not intrinsic affinity, reliability, function probabilities, target transfer or annotation credit. |

Resource meanings preserve supply basis, coefficient-to-demand linkage, period and compartment. Missing descriptions add no numerical gate. These are conditional source comparisons and fixed-consumption constraints, not extra assays, physiological sufficiency, target correspondence or annotation credit. |
| literature_structure_cells | Saved conditional source-model geometry and local residue RSCC components with exact source bindings. Entry/revision validation, fixed required panels, proposed physical predicates and unreported coordinate/measurement uncertainty remain explicit. Conditional state/assignment identities do not establish population occupancy, causal isolation or target annotation credit. |
| literature_transport_cells | Saved native concentrations and conditional same-curve kinetic-response components. condition_response is test substrate saturation divided by reference substrate saturation under the same fitted curve. Keep actual curve/preparation identities, Km uncertainty meaning and full covariance ownership. The response can divide an appropriate source effect once; it is not reliability or annotation credit. Missing or different curves remain unavailable. |
